Full handout details — topic/content එක අඩු කරලා නැහැ
Designing the Brand Mantra

A mantra combines function, description, and emotion.

KEY CONCEPT Brand Functions This part states what the brand provides. It describes the product or service nature. It can describe the experience provided. It can describe the benefit provided.
📌 EXAMPLE Example: “Performance” describes what Nike helps customers achieve.
KEY CONCEPT Descriptive Modifier This word further clarifies the brand function. It narrows the function’s meaning. It states the type of product or experience. It makes the mantra more specific.
📌 EXAMPLE Example: “Athletic” clarifies Nike’s performance function.
KEY CONCEPT Emotional Modifier This word explains how the brand provides the benefit. It adds emotional character. It guides the tone of brand delivery. It helps employees understand the desired feeling.
📌 EXAMPLE Example: “Authentic” explains how Nike should deliver athletic performance.
🔗 Link to Previous Slide: Slide 70 defined a brand mantra. This slide explains its three building parts.
SLIDE 72 — Brand Mantra Examples
Famous brands combine emotional, descriptive, and functional words.
සිංහල idea
Brand Mantra කියන්නේ brand එකේ inner heart එක words 3–5කින් කියන short internal guide එක. Usually emotional + descriptive + functional idea combine කරනවා. Slogan එක customer-facing; mantra එක company ඇතුළේ strategy guide.
Simple English idea: Famous brands combine emotional, descriptive, and functional words.
Full handout details — topic/content එක අඩු කරලා නැහැ
Brand Mantra Examples

Famous brands combine emotional, descriptive, and functional words.

KEY CONCEPT Three-Part Brand Mantras Read every example from left to right.
📌 EXAMPLE Example: “Fun family entertainment” gives Disney a clear internal direction.
EXAM FOCUS How to Build One Choose one word for each mantra role. Start with the brand function. Add a descriptive modifier. Add an emotional modifier. Keep the final phrase short and clear.
📌 EXAMPLE Example: A tea brand could use “Warm Sri Lankan refreshment.”
🔗 Link to Previous Slide: Slide 71 explained the three parts. This final slide applies them to real brands.

Last 40 Minutes — මේක විතරක් අන්තිමට බලන්න

Past papers 2023, 2024, 2025 සහ handouts එකට බලලා, exam එකට application/theory answer එකක් හදන්න අන්තිම 40 minutes වල refresh කරගන්න ඕන core concepts මෙතනම එකට දාලා තියෙනවා. Past paper list එකක් නෙමෙයි — බලන්න ඕන theories ටිකම.

Answer rule: term එක මතක නම් term එක දාන්න. term එක අමතක නම් meaning එක simple English/Sinhala logic එකෙන් explain කරලා example එකක් දෙන්න. 6–8 marks නම් meaning + points 3–5 + example.

0–6 min — ATTITUDE

Tri-Component
Cognitive = thinking/knowledge. Affective = feeling/emotion. Conative = action/intention. Memory: THINK → FEEL → ACT.
Attitude Change 5
1 Need එක target කරන්න. 2 Admired people/group එක්ක connect කරන්න. 3 Conflicting negative idea fix කරන්න. 4 Product judge කරන attributes/beliefs change කරන්න. 5 Competitor ගැන belief change කරන්න.
Reasoned Action
What result will happen? → good/bad ද? + important people මොනවද හිතන්නේ? → ඔවුන්ට කොච්චර listen කරනවද? → own attitude + social pressure → intention → behaviour.
Cognitive Dissonance
Purchase එකෙන් පස්සේ 'මම හරි choice එකද කළේ?' කියන doubt/regret. Warranty, after-sales service, reassurance, good reviews දීලා reduce කරන්න.
Attribution
Result එකේ reason එක කාට/මොකට දාන්නේද? Product/companyටද, තමන්ටද, situation එකටද කියලා explain කරන theory.

6–12 min — PERSONALITY

Freudian / Neo-Freudian / Trait
Freudian = hidden unconscious/biological drives. Neo-Freudian = social relationships shape personality. Trait = measurable psychological characteristics.
Dogmatism
Mind open ද closed ද? High = new/unfamiliar idea reject කරන්න tendency. Low = flexible.
Innovativeness
New product ඉක්මනින් try කරන්න willingness.
OSL / Sensation / Variety
OSL = how much newness/excitement you like. Sensation Seeking = strong new experience + risk. Variety Seeking = satisfied උනත් change/newness ඕන.
Variety 3
Exploratory = change එකකට new brand BUY. Vicarious = BUY නෑ, info SEARCH. Use Innovativeness = already-owned product NEW WAY එකකට use.
NFC / Visualizer / Verbalizer
High NFC = details/specs. Low NFC = simple/celebrity/music. Visualizer = images/video. Verbalizer = written words.
Materialism / Fixated / Compulsive / Ethnocentrism
Materialism = buy + show off + want more. Fixated = one category obsession. Compulsive = can't stop buying. Ethnocentrism = foreign avoid, local prefer.

12–18 min — LEARNING + PERCEPTION

Classical Conditioning
Neutral signal එක strong positive/known stimulus එක්ක repeatedly pair කරලා signal එක තනියම same response trigger කරනවා.
Repetition / Generalization / Discrimination
Repetition = repeat message. Generalization = same response similar products. Discrimination = brand එක competitorsගෙන් clearly different කියලා learn.
Operant Conditioning
Try product → good result/reward → repeat. Bad result → stop/avoid.
Reinforcement
Positive = good reward add. Negative = bad thing avoid කරන්න behaviour encourage. Punishment = bad consequence දී behaviour reduce. Extinction = reward stop වෙලා behaviour fade.
Threshold 3
Absolute = minimum to NOTICE. JND = minimum CHANGE to notice. Subliminal = below conscious awareness.
Selective 3
Exposure = ad choose/skip. Attention = ad there, relevant එකට focus. Defense = uncomfortable message brain block.
Gestalt
Figure-ground = main vs background. Grouping = similar/close things group. Closure = missing part brain fill.
Halo Effect
One strong feature/impression whole brand/product judgement එකට spread වෙනවා.

18–24 min — MOTIVATION

Motivation Process
Unfulfilled Need → Tension → Drive → Behavior → Goal/Need Fulfillment → Tension Reduction. Previous learning + thinking behaviour එකට බලපානවා.
Need Types
Innate = born/body. Acquired = learned/social. Intrinsic = inner satisfaction. Extrinsic = outside reward/status/avoid punishment.
Goals
Generic vs product-specific. Positive goal = move toward good result. Negative goal = move away from bad result.
Rational vs Emotional
Rational = price/size/performance. Emotional = pride/love/fear/status.
Substitute / Frustration / Defense
Main goal reach බැරි → substitute goal. Goal blocked → frustration. Mind protect කරන්න aggression, rationalization, withdrawal, projection, daydreaming etc.
Arousal 4
Physiological = body. Emotional = feelings. Cognitive = thinking/info. Environmental = ad/discount/surroundings.
Maslow
Physiological → Safety → Belonging → Esteem → Self-Actualization.

24–30 min — FAMILY + REFERENCE GROUPS

Consumer Socialization
Children consumer skills, knowledge, attitudes, experience learn කරන process. Family major agent.
Children as 3 Markets
Primary = child buys now. Influence = parents decision influence now. Future = future customer.
Family Decisions
Husband-dominated / Wife-dominated / Joint / Autonomic. Ask: final say කාගෙද?
Buying Roles
Influencer / Gatekeeper / Decider / Buyer / User (+ maintainer/disposer).
Normative vs Comparative
Normative = belong/acceptance නිසා group rules follow. Comparative = admire කරන person/group copy.
Membership vs Symbolic
Membership = real group join/belong. Symbolic = admire but unrealistic to join.
Source Credibility
Expertise + Trustworthiness + Believability.
Opinion Leader / E-WOM
Opinion leader specific area advice දෙයි. E-WOM online reviews/posts/videos. Trust depends on closeness, similarity, credibility.

30–35 min — PERSUASION + CULTURE

Traditional vs New Media
Traditional = broad, mostly one-way, less targeted. New = interactive, targeted, measurable, shareable.
Framing
Positive = gain/save. Negative = loss/miss. Same fact, different angle.
One vs Two-Sided
One-sided = good only. Two-sided = good + limitation/other side.
Communication Flow
Sender → Encode → Message → Channel → Receiver → Decode → Feedback.
Culture
Shared values/beliefs/norms/customs that silently guide consumer behaviour.
Symbols / Heroes / Rituals / Values
Culture parts: symbol = meaning sign; hero = admired person; ritual = repeated meaningful action; value = deep belief.
Enculturation / Acculturation
Own culture learn / other culture learn-adapt.
Hofstede Quick
Power Distance = power gap; Individualism/Collectivism = ME/WE; Uncertainty Avoidance = risk/unknown comfort; Masculinity/Femininity = win/status vs care/life quality; Long/Short = future vs now/tradition; Indulgence/Restraint = enjoy vs control.

35–40 min — INTRO + BRAND POSITIONING EMERGENCY

STP
Segmentation = groups. Targeting = choose group. Positioning = clear place/meaning in customer mind.
4Ps
Product / Price / Place / Promotion.
Marketing Myopia
Product එකට focus කරලා customer need එක අමතක කරන එක.
Value / Satisfaction / Retention
Value = benefits vs resources. Satisfaction = performance vs expectation. Retention = customer comes back.
Decision Model
Input → Process → Output.
Segmentation Bases
Geographic / Demographic / Psychographic / Behavioral.
POP / POD
POP = category fit/expected similarity. POD = strong unique reason to choose.
Frame / Perceptual Map
Frame = who customer + who competitors + same/different. Perceptual map = customer mind එකේ brands compare කරන 2-axis map.
Positioning Statement / Slogan / Mantra
Statement = full internal strategy sentence. Slogan = public short line. Mantra = 3–5 word internal brand heart.

Special Words

Original exam term + a normal daily-English meaning + Sinhala memory cue. If the exact term disappears in the exam, the “write this idea” line helps you still explain the concept clearly.

01. Introduction

Consumer Behavior
how people search, choose, buy, use and react to products
මිනිස්සු product එකක් හොයන, තෝරන, ගන්න, use කරන විදිහ
If you forget “Consumer Behavior”, write the idea: how people search, choose, buy, use and react to products.
Marketing Concept
start from the customer need, not from the product
මුලින් customerට ඕනේ මොකක්ද බලලා business එක හදන එක
If you forget “Marketing Concept”, write the idea: start from the customer need, not from the product.
Production Concept
make a lot and make it easy/cheap to get
ගොඩක් හදලා ලේසියෙන්/අඩුවට දෙන්න
If you forget “Production Concept”, write the idea: make a lot and make it easy/cheap to get.
Product Concept
make the best quality product
quality එක හොඳම එක කරන්න
If you forget “Product Concept”, write the idea: make the best quality product.
Selling Concept
push hard to sell what the company already made
හදපු දේ විකුණන්න තදින් push කරන එක
If you forget “Selling Concept”, write the idea: push hard to sell what the company already made.
Marketing Myopia
loving the product but forgetting the real customer need
product එකම බලලා customer need එක අමතක කරන එක
If you forget “Marketing Myopia”, write the idea: loving the product but forgetting the real customer need.
Segmentation
split one big market into smaller similar groups
ලොකු market එක සමාන කට්ටියට කඩන එක
If you forget “Segmentation”, write the idea: split one big market into smaller similar groups.
Targeting
choose the group you want to serve
serve කරන්න ඕනේ group එක තෝරන එක
If you forget “Targeting”, write the idea: choose the group you want to serve.
Positioning
put a clear idea about the brand in the customer's mind
brand එක customerගේ ඔලුවේ clear place එකකට දාන එක
If you forget “Positioning”, write the idea: put a clear idea about the brand in the customer's mind.
Customer Value
what I get compared with money, time and effort I give
මම දෙන සල්ලි/වෙලාවට මට ලැබෙන දේ වටිනවද
If you forget “Customer Value”, write the idea: what I get compared with money, time and effort I give.
Customer Satisfaction
what I got compared with what I expected
මං බලාපොරොත්තු වුණ දේට ලැබුණ දේ ගැලපෙනවද
If you forget “Customer Satisfaction”, write the idea: what I got compared with what I expected.
Customer Retention
keep old customers coming back
පරණ customerලා ආයෙත් ගන්න එන්න තියාගන්න එක
If you forget “Customer Retention”, write the idea: keep old customers coming back.
Emotional Bond
customer stays because of feelings and attachment
හිතේ බැඳීම නිසා brand එකත් එක්ක ඉන්න එක
If you forget “Emotional Bond”, write the idea: customer stays because of feelings and attachment.
Transactional Bond
customer stays because of price, points, offers or convenience
offer/points/price වගේ practical benefit නිසා ඉන්න එක
If you forget “Transactional Bond”, write the idea: customer stays because of price, points, offers or convenience.
Consumer Decision Model
input → think/decide → buy/use/result
information එනවා → හිතනවා → තීරණය කරලා කරනවා
If you forget “Consumer Decision Model”, write the idea: input → think/decide → buy/use/result.

02. Consumer Perception

Perception
the meaning your brain gives to what you see, hear, smell, touch or taste
පිටින් එන information එකට මොලේ meaning එක දෙන එක
If you forget “Perception”, write the idea: the meaning your brain gives to what you see, hear, smell, touch or taste.
Sensation
the first quick reaction of the senses
ඇස/කන/නාසය වගේ sense එකට මුලින්ම දැනෙන එක
If you forget “Sensation”, write the idea: the first quick reaction of the senses.
Stimulus
anything that enters one of your senses
sense එකකට එන ඕනෑම signal එක
If you forget “Stimulus”, write the idea: anything that enters one of your senses.
Sensory Receptors
eyes, ears, nose, tongue and skin that receive signals
ඇස කන නාසය දිව සම
If you forget “Sensory Receptors”, write the idea: eyes, ears, nose, tongue and skin that receive signals.
Absolute Threshold
the smallest level needed to notice something exists
දෙයක් තියෙනවා කියලා notice වෙන්න ඕනේ අඩුම level එක
If you forget “Absolute Threshold”, write the idea: the smallest level needed to notice something exists.
Differential Threshold
the smallest change you can notice
වෙනසක් තේරෙන්න ඕනේ අඩුම change එක
If you forget “Differential Threshold”, write the idea: the smallest change you can notice.
JND (Just Noticeable Difference)
the minimum noticeable change
notice වෙන්න තරම් පොඩිම වෙනස
If you forget “JND (Just Noticeable Difference)”, write the idea: the minimum noticeable change.
Weber's Law
bigger original stimulus needs a bigger change to notice
මුල් එක ලොකු නම් change එකත් ලොකු වෙන්න ඕනේ
If you forget “Weber's Law”, write the idea: bigger original stimulus needs a bigger change to notice.
Subliminal Perception
your brain gets a message but you are not consciously aware
ඔයාට දැනෙන්නේ නැතිව මොලේ message එක ගන්න එක
If you forget “Subliminal Perception”, write the idea: your brain gets a message but you are not consciously aware.
Perceptual Selection
we choose only some information to notice
හැමදේම නෙමෙයි, ටිකක් විතරක් notice කරන එක
If you forget “Perceptual Selection”, write the idea: we choose only some information to notice.
Selective Exposure
you choose which messages you will face/watch
කැමති ad/message එක බලනවා; අකමැති එක skip කරනවා
If you forget “Selective Exposure”, write the idea: you choose which messages you will face/watch.
Selective Attention
message is there, but you give attention only to what matters to you
ad එක තියෙනවා; ඔයාට වැදගත් එකට විතරක් අවධානය යනවා
If you forget “Selective Attention”, write the idea: message is there, but you give attention only to what matters to you.
Perceptual Defense
the mind blocks uncomfortable or threatening information
අසහනකාරී message එක මොලෙන් block කරන එක
If you forget “Perceptual Defense”, write the idea: the mind blocks uncomfortable or threatening information.
Gestalt Psychology
the brain joins pieces and sees one whole picture
කෑලි ටික එකට එකතු කරලා මොලේ complete picture එක බලන එක
If you forget “Gestalt Psychology”, write the idea: the brain joins pieces and sees one whole picture.
Figure and Ground
main thing stands out; rest becomes background
main object එක figure; අනිත් එක background
If you forget “Figure and Ground”, write the idea: main thing stands out; rest becomes background.
Grouping
brain puts similar/near things together
සමාන/ළඟ දේවල් එක group එකක් වගේ බලන එක
If you forget “Grouping”, write the idea: brain puts similar/near things together.
Closure
brain fills missing parts and completes the picture
අඩු කෑල්ල මොලෙන් පුරවලා full image එක හදාගන්න එක
If you forget “Closure”, write the idea: brain fills missing parts and completes the picture.
Stereotyping
quickly judging using a ready-made idea about a group
group එකක් ගැන කලින් idea එකෙන් ඉක්මනට judge කරන එක
If you forget “Stereotyping”, write the idea: quickly judging using a ready-made idea about a group.
First Impression
the first idea strongly affects later judgement
මුලින් හිතපු දේ පස්සෙත් බලපාන එක
If you forget “First Impression”, write the idea: the first idea strongly affects later judgement.
Halo Effect
one strong good/bad feature changes how we judge everything else
එක හොඳ/නරක feature එකෙන් මුළු brand එකම judge කරන එක
If you forget “Halo Effect”, write the idea: one strong good/bad feature changes how we judge everything else.

03. Consumer Motivation

Need
something missing that a person wants to satisfy
අඩුවක්/අවශ්‍යතාවයක්
If you forget “Need”, write the idea: something missing that a person wants to satisfy.
Motivation
inner push that makes a person act
කරන්න තල්ලු කරන ඇතුළේ බලය
If you forget “Motivation”, write the idea: inner push that makes a person act.
Tension
uncomfortable feeling because a need is not satisfied
need එක satisfy නැති නිසා ඇති වෙන pressure එක
If you forget “Tension”, write the idea: uncomfortable feeling because a need is not satisfied.
Drive
urge to do something to reduce tension
tension එක අඩු කරන්න action ගන්න urge එක
If you forget “Drive”, write the idea: urge to do something to reduce tension.
Innate Needs
needs we are born with
උපන්දා ඉඳන් තියෙන needs — food, water වගේ
If you forget “Innate Needs”, write the idea: needs we are born with.
Acquired Needs
needs learned from society and life
සමාජයෙන් ඉගෙනගත් needs — status, power වගේ
If you forget “Acquired Needs”, write the idea: needs learned from society and life.
Extrinsic Motivation
do it for an outside reward or to avoid punishment
පිටින් ලැබෙන reward/penalty නිසා කරන එක
If you forget “Extrinsic Motivation”, write the idea: do it for an outside reward or to avoid punishment.
Intrinsic Motivation
do it because you personally enjoy it
තමන්ටම ආස නිසා කරන එක
If you forget “Intrinsic Motivation”, write the idea: do it because you personally enjoy it.
Generic Goal
general way to satisfy a need
need එක satisfy කරන general goal එක
If you forget “Generic Goal”, write the idea: general way to satisfy a need.
Product-Specific Goal
a specific brand/product chosen to satisfy the need
specifically Coca-Cola/Apple වගේ එකක් තෝරන goal එක
If you forget “Product-Specific Goal”, write the idea: a specific brand/product chosen to satisfy the need.
Positive Goal (Approach)
move toward something you want
කැමති දේකට ළඟා වෙන එක
If you forget “Positive Goal (Approach)”, write the idea: move toward something you want.
Negative Goal (Avoidance)
move away from something bad
නරක දෙයක් avoid කරන එක
If you forget “Negative Goal (Avoidance)”, write the idea: move away from something bad.
Rational Motive
buy using facts like price, size and value
logic/facts බලලා ගන්න එක
If you forget “Rational Motive”, write the idea: buy using facts like price, size and value.
Emotional Motive
buy using feelings like love, pride, fear or status
feeling එකෙන් ගන්න එක
If you forget “Emotional Motive”, write the idea: buy using feelings like love, pride, fear or status.
Substitute Goal
replacement goal when the first goal cannot be reached
මුල් goal එක බැරි උනාම වෙන එකක් තෝරන එක
If you forget “Substitute Goal”, write the idea: replacement goal when the first goal cannot be reached.
Frustration
bad feeling when a goal cannot be reached
goal එක fail උනාම ඇති වෙන upset/අසහනය
If you forget “Frustration”, write the idea: bad feeling when a goal cannot be reached.
Defense Mechanism
mental trick used to protect feelings after failure
fail උනාම හිත protect කරගන්න මොලේ කරන trick එක
If you forget “Defense Mechanism”, write the idea: mental trick used to protect feelings after failure.
Aggression
show anger because of frustration
frustration එක කේන්තියෙන් පිට කරන එක
If you forget “Aggression”, write the idea: show anger because of frustration.
Rationalization
make a nice-sounding excuse for failure
fail උන එකට හොඳ excuse එකක් හදාගන්න එක
If you forget “Rationalization”, write the idea: make a nice-sounding excuse for failure.
Regression
go back to childish behaviour
පොඩි ළමයෙක් වගේ behave කරන එක
If you forget “Regression”, write the idea: go back to childish behaviour.
Withdrawal
give up and avoid the situation
අතහැරලා අයින් වෙන එක
If you forget “Withdrawal”, write the idea: give up and avoid the situation.
Projection
blame another person for your own problem
තමන්ගේ වැරැද්ද වෙන කෙනෙක්ට දාන එක
If you forget “Projection”, write the idea: blame another person for your own problem.
Daydreaming
escape into fantasy instead of facing the problem
problem එකට face නොවී හිතෙන් වෙන ලෝකෙකට යන එක
If you forget “Daydreaming”, write the idea: escape into fantasy instead of facing the problem.
Identification
copy a successful person to feel better
successful කෙනෙක් copy කරන එක
If you forget “Identification”, write the idea: copy a successful person to feel better.
Repression
push a painful memory out of conscious thinking
නරක memory එක මතකෙන් තල්ලු කරලා නොහිතා ඉන්න එක
If you forget “Repression”, write the idea: push a painful memory out of conscious thinking.
Arousal of Motives
what switches a need/motive on
motivation එක ON කරන trigger එක
If you forget “Arousal of Motives”, write the idea: what switches a need/motive on.
Maslow's Hierarchy
needs move from basic survival to higher personal growth
basic need ඉඳන් ඉහළ needs වලට යන pyramid එක
If you forget “Maslow's Hierarchy”, write the idea: needs move from basic survival to higher personal growth.
Trio of Needs
power + affiliation + achievement
බලය + කට්ටියත් එක්ක ඉන්න ඕනේ + ජයගන්න ඕනේ
If you forget “Trio of Needs”, write the idea: power + affiliation + achievement.
Projective Techniques
indirect ways to discover hidden motives
කෙලින් අහන්නේ නැතුව hidden reason හොයන methods
If you forget “Projective Techniques”, write the idea: indirect ways to discover hidden motives.
TAT
show a picture and ask the person to make a story
picture එකක් දීලා story එකෙන් hidden motive හොයන එක
If you forget “TAT”, write the idea: show a picture and ask the person to make a story.

04. Personality

Personality
inner mental qualities that make people behave differently
එක් එක් කෙනා වෙනස් විදිහට හිතන්න/කරන්න හේතු වන ඇතුළේ traits
If you forget “Personality”, write the idea: inner mental qualities that make people behave differently.
Freudian Theory
hidden unconscious and biological drives shape behavior
යටි හිතේ hidden wants/biological drives නිසා behavior හැදෙනවා
If you forget “Freudian Theory”, write the idea: hidden unconscious and biological drives shape behavior.
Id
wants pleasure now
දැන්ම ඕනේ කියන හිතේ කොටස
If you forget “Id”, write the idea: wants pleasure now.
Ego
tries to make a realistic decision
real world එක බලලා practical decision ගන්න කොටස
If you forget “Ego”, write the idea: tries to make a realistic decision.
Superego
says what is right or wrong
හරි/වැරදි කියන conscience එක
If you forget “Superego”, write the idea: says what is right or wrong.
Neo-Freudian Theory
social relationships also shape personality
friends/family/social relationships නිසා personality හැදෙනවා
If you forget “Neo-Freudian Theory”, write the idea: social relationships also shape personality.
Trait Theory
measure personality using specific traits/scores
personality එක measurable traits වලින් බලන එක
If you forget “Trait Theory”, write the idea: measure personality using specific traits/scores.
Consumer Innovativeness
how willing someone is to try new products early
අලුත් product එක ඉක්මනට try කරන්න කොච්චර කැමතිද
If you forget “Consumer Innovativeness”, write the idea: how willing someone is to try new products early.
Dogmatism
how closed or open the mind is to new ideas
අලුත් idea එකකට mind එක කොච්චර open/closed ද
If you forget “Dogmatism”, write the idea: how closed or open the mind is to new ideas.
Social Character
whether a person follows inner values or other people's views
තමන්ගේ හිත අහනවද, අනිත් අය අහනවද
If you forget “Social Character”, write the idea: whether a person follows inner values or other people's views.
Need for Uniqueness
want to be different from other people
අනිත් අයට වඩා වෙනස් වෙන්න ඕනේ
If you forget “Need for Uniqueness”, write the idea: want to be different from other people.
Optimum Stimulation Level (OSL)
how much newness/excitement a person likes
කෙනෙක්ට කොච්චර new/exciting දේවල් ඕනේද
If you forget “Optimum Stimulation Level (OSL)”, write the idea: how much newness/excitement a person likes.
Sensation Seeking
likes strong, new and sometimes risky experiences
new + strong + risk තියෙන experience වලට ආස
If you forget “Sensation Seeking”, write the idea: likes strong, new and sometimes risky experiences.
Variety & Novelty Seeking
changes products even when already satisfied
satisfied උනත් වෙනසකට අලුත් එකක් try කරන එක
If you forget “Variety & Novelty Seeking”, write the idea: changes products even when already satisfied.
Exploratory Purchase Behavior
actually buy a different product just for change
වෙනසකට අලුත් brand එකක් buy කරන එක
If you forget “Exploratory Purchase Behavior”, write the idea: actually buy a different product just for change.
Vicarious Exploration
search and read about new things without buying
buy කරන්නේ නැහැ; info හොයනවා
If you forget “Vicarious Exploration”, write the idea: search and read about new things without buying.
Use Innovativeness
use an existing product in a new way
තියෙන product එක වෙනස් වැඩකට use කරන එක
If you forget “Use Innovativeness”, write the idea: use an existing product in a new way.
Need for Cognition (NFC)
how much a person enjoys deep thinking
details/specs බලලා හිතන්න කොච්චර ආසද
If you forget “Need for Cognition (NFC)”, write the idea: how much a person enjoys deep thinking.
Visualizer
prefers photos, video and graphics
photo/video/graphics වලින් info ගන්න ආස
If you forget “Visualizer”, write the idea: prefers photos, video and graphics.
Verbalizer
prefers written words and detailed text
text/details කියවලා info ගන්න ආස
If you forget “Verbalizer”, write the idea: prefers written words and detailed text.
Consumer Materialism
giving high importance to owning and showing possessions
දේවල් buy කරලා show off කරන එකට වැඩි value දෙන එක
If you forget “Consumer Materialism”, write the idea: giving high importance to owning and showing possessions.
Fixated Consumption
very strong interest in one product/category
එක category එකකට ගොඩක් fix වෙලා collect/buy කරන එක
If you forget “Fixated Consumption”, write the idea: very strong interest in one product/category.
Compulsive Consumption
cannot control or stop buying
buy කරන එක නවත්තගන්න බැරි තත්ත්වය
If you forget “Compulsive Consumption”, write the idea: cannot control or stop buying.
Consumer Ethnocentrism
believing own-country products should be preferred
තමන්ගේ රටේ product හොඳයි/ඒවා ගන්න ඕනේ කියන strong belief එක
If you forget “Consumer Ethnocentrism”, write the idea: believing own-country products should be preferred.
Brand Personality
human-like character people give to a brand
brand එක මනුස්සයෙක් වගේ character එකක් තියෙනවා කියලා හිතන එක
If you forget “Brand Personality”, write the idea: human-like character people give to a brand.
Sincerity
honest / real
honest
If you forget “Sincerity”, write the idea: honest / real.
Excitement
energy / fun
energy
If you forget “Excitement”, write the idea: energy / fun.
Competence
capable / trusted
වැඩේ කරන්න පුළුවන් / trustable
If you forget “Competence”, write the idea: capable / trusted.
Sophistication
elegant / classy
elegant / classy
If you forget “Sophistication”, write the idea: elegant / classy.
Ruggedness
strong / tough
strong / tough
If you forget “Ruggedness”, write the idea: strong / tough.

05. Consumer Learning

Consumer Learning
gain knowledge/experience and use it in future buying
ඉස්සරහට buy කරන්න knowledge + experience ගන්න එක
If you forget “Consumer Learning”, write the idea: gain knowledge/experience and use it in future buying.
Behavioral Learning
learn from a trigger and visible response
stimulus එකකට response එකෙන් ඉගෙනගන්න එක
If you forget “Behavioral Learning”, write the idea: learn from a trigger and visible response.
Cognitive Learning
learn by thinking, reasoning and problem solving
හිතලා/compare කරලා ඉගෙනගන්න එක
If you forget “Cognitive Learning”, write the idea: learn by thinking, reasoning and problem solving.
Classical Conditioning
pair a neutral thing with a strong trigger until the neutral thing creates the feeling
reaction නැති signal එක strong feeling එකක් එක්ක repeat කරලා ඒ signal එකටත් feeling එක එන එක
If you forget “Classical Conditioning”, write the idea: pair a neutral thing with a strong trigger until the neutral thing creates the feeling.
Unconditioned Stimulus (US)
natural trigger
naturally reaction එක ගන්න trigger එක
If you forget “Unconditioned Stimulus (US)”, write the idea: natural trigger.
Unconditioned Response (UR)
natural reaction
natural response එක
If you forget “Unconditioned Response (UR)”, write the idea: natural reaction.
Conditioned Stimulus (CS)
old neutral signal that becomes meaningful after pairing
මුලින් neutral; pairing පස්සේ meaning ඇති signal එක
If you forget “Conditioned Stimulus (CS)”, write the idea: old neutral signal that becomes meaningful after pairing.
Conditioned Response (CR)
learned reaction to the conditioned signal
learned response එක
If you forget “Conditioned Response (CR)”, write the idea: learned reaction to the conditioned signal.
Repetition
show the same message again and again
repeat කරන එක
If you forget “Repetition”, write the idea: show the same message again and again.
Stimulus Generalization
respond similarly to similar products/signals
එක product එකේ trust එක similar product වලටත් දාන එක
If you forget “Stimulus Generalization”, write the idea: respond similarly to similar products/signals.
Product Line Extension
new version inside the same product line
same product එකේ new flavor/size වගේ
If you forget “Product Line Extension”, write the idea: new version inside the same product line.
Product Form Extension
same basic product in a different form
tablet → syrup වගේ form එක වෙනස් කරන එක
If you forget “Product Form Extension”, write the idea: same basic product in a different form.
Product Category Extension
same brand enters a new category
brand trust එක අරගෙන වෙන category එකකට යන එක
If you forget “Product Category Extension”, write the idea: same brand enters a new category.
Stimulus Discrimination
teach people to see your brand as different from similar brands
competitors අතරින් තමන් වෙනස් කියලා clear කරන එක
If you forget “Stimulus Discrimination”, write the idea: teach people to see your brand as different from similar brands.
Operant / Instrumental Conditioning
learn by trying; good result = repeat, bad result = stop
try කරනවා; result හොඳ නම් ආයෙ කරනවා
If you forget “Operant / Instrumental Conditioning”, write the idea: learn by trying; good result = repeat, bad result = stop.
Positive Reinforcement
give a good reward so behavior repeats
reward එකක් දීලා ආයෙත් ඒ action එක කරවන්න
If you forget “Positive Reinforcement”, write the idea: give a good reward so behavior repeats.
Negative Reinforcement
act to avoid/remove a bad outcome
නරක දෙයක් avoid කරන්න action කරන එක
If you forget “Negative Reinforcement”, write the idea: act to avoid/remove a bad outcome.
Punishment
add a bad consequence to reduce a behavior
behavior එක අඩු කරන්න bad result එකක් දෙන එක
If you forget “Punishment”, write the idea: add a bad consequence to reduce a behavior.
Extinction
learned behavior fades when reward/connection stops
reward/connection නැති උනාම behavior එක හෙමින් නවතින එක
If you forget “Extinction”, write the idea: learned behavior fades when reward/connection stops.
Reinforcement Schedule
the pattern/timing used to give rewards
reward දෙන pattern එක
If you forget “Reinforcement Schedule”, write the idea: the pattern/timing used to give rewards.
Observational / Vicarious Learning
learn by watching what happens to other people
අනිත් අය බලලා ඉගෙනගන්න එක
If you forget “Observational / Vicarious Learning”, write the idea: learn by watching what happens to other people.
Brand Loyalty
keep choosing the same brand again and again
එකම brand එක නැවත නැවත තෝරන එක
If you forget “Brand Loyalty”, write the idea: keep choosing the same brand again and again.

06. Consumer Attitude

Attitude
learned like/dislike feeling about a product, brand or idea
product/brand එකක් ගැන ඉගෙනගත් කැමැත්ත/අකමැත්ත
If you forget “Attitude”, write the idea: learned like/dislike feeling about a product, brand or idea.
Attitude Object
the thing the attitude is about
attitude එක තියෙන්නේ මොන දේ ගැනද
If you forget “Attitude Object”, write the idea: the thing the attitude is about.
Tri-Component Attitude Model
attitude has thinking + feeling + action
thinking + feeling + action තුන
If you forget “Tri-Component Attitude Model”, write the idea: attitude has thinking + feeling + action.
Cognitive Component
thinking, knowledge and beliefs
හිතන දේ + දන්න දේ
If you forget “Cognitive Component”, write the idea: thinking, knowledge and beliefs.
Affective Component
feelings and emotions
හැඟීම්
If you forget “Affective Component”, write the idea: feelings and emotions.
Conative Component
intention, action and behavior
කරන්න හිතන/කරන දේ
If you forget “Conative Component”, write the idea: intention, action and behavior.
Multi-Attribute Model
judge a brand using several important features
features කිහිපයක් බලලා brand එක judge කරන එක
If you forget “Multi-Attribute Model”, write the idea: judge a brand using several important features.
Attitude-Toward-Object Model
how you feel about the product/brand itself
product/brand එක ගැන attitude එක
If you forget “Attitude-Toward-Object Model”, write the idea: how you feel about the product/brand itself.
Attitude-Toward-Behavior Model
how you feel about doing the action, like buying or using
buy/use කරන action එක ගැන attitude එක
If you forget “Attitude-Toward-Behavior Model”, write the idea: how you feel about doing the action, like buying or using.
Theory of Reasoned Action
your attitude + social pressure → intention → behavior
මං හිතන දේ + අනිත් අයගේ pressure → plan → action
If you forget “Theory of Reasoned Action”, write the idea: your attitude + social pressure → intention → behavior.
Beliefs About Outcomes
what you think will happen if you do the action
action එක කළොත් මොකක් වෙයිද
If you forget “Beliefs About Outcomes”, write the idea: what you think will happen if you do the action.
Evaluation of Outcomes
decide if that result is good or bad
result එක හොඳද නරකද
If you forget “Evaluation of Outcomes”, write the idea: decide if that result is good or bad.
Beliefs About Referents
what important people think you should do
අනිත් වැදගත් අය මොකක් කරන්න කියයිද
If you forget “Beliefs About Referents”, write the idea: what important people think you should do.
Motivation to Comply
how much you want to listen to those people
එයාලට කොච්චර අහනවද
If you forget “Motivation to Comply”, write the idea: how much you want to listen to those people.
Subjective Norm
social pressure from other people's views
අනිත් අයගෙන් එන social pressure එක
If you forget “Subjective Norm”, write the idea: social pressure from other people's views.
Theory of Trying to Consume
focus on trying when success is not guaranteed
result sure නැති වෙලාවේ try කරන process එක බලන එක
If you forget “Theory of Trying to Consume”, write the idea: focus on trying when success is not guaranteed.
Attitude-Toward-the-Ad Model
feelings about the ad can change feelings about the brand
ad එක ගැන feeling එක brand එකටත් යනවා
If you forget “Attitude-Toward-the-Ad Model”, write the idea: feelings about the ad can change feelings about the brand.
Changing Basic Motivational Function
change attitude by connecting the product to an important need
customerගේ main need එක target කරලා attitude වෙනස් කරන එක
If you forget “Changing Basic Motivational Function”, write the idea: change attitude by connecting the product to an important need.
Utilitarian Function
show that the product is useful
useful කියලා පෙන්නන එක
If you forget “Utilitarian Function”, write the idea: show that the product is useful.
Ego-Defensive Function
help the person feel safe/confident
confidence/security feeling එක දෙන එක
If you forget “Ego-Defensive Function”, write the idea: help the person feel safe/confident.
Value-Expressive Function
connect product with the person's values/lifestyle
එයාගේ values/lifestyle එකට product එක connect කරන එක
If you forget “Value-Expressive Function”, write the idea: connect product with the person's values/lifestyle.
Knowledge Function
give useful information and understanding
දැනුම/clarity දෙන එක
If you forget “Knowledge Function”, write the idea: give useful information and understanding.
Admired Group Association
connect the product with people/groups the customer likes
customer කැමති කෙනෙක්/group එකක් brand එකට connect කරන එක
If you forget “Admired Group Association”, write the idea: connect the product with people/groups the customer likes.
Resolving Conflicting Attitudes
remove a conflict between two feelings/ideas
ඔලුවේ තියෙන දෙකක් අතර conflict එක fix කරන එක
If you forget “Resolving Conflicting Attitudes”, write the idea: remove a conflict between two feelings/ideas.
Cognitive Dissonance
regret/doubt after making a decision or purchase
දෙයක් ගත්තට පස්සේ “මං හරිද කරේ?” කියන doubt/regret එක
If you forget “Cognitive Dissonance”, write the idea: regret/doubt after making a decision or purchase.
Attribution Theory
how people explain the reason for an action/result
මේ result/action එකට හේතුව මොකක්ද කියලා explain කරන විදිහ
If you forget “Attribution Theory”, write the idea: how people explain the reason for an action/result.

07. Family & Social Standing

Family
people related by blood, marriage or adoption living together
ලේ/විවාහ/දරුකමෙන් සම්බන්ධ අය එකට ඉන්න එක
If you forget “Family”, write the idea: people related by blood, marriage or adoption living together.
Household
people living together; they do not have to be family
එක ගෙදර ඉන්න අය; family වෙන්නම ඕනේ නැහැ
If you forget “Household”, write the idea: people living together; they do not have to be family.
Nuclear Family
parents + children
අම්මා තාත්තා ළමයි
If you forget “Nuclear Family”, write the idea: parents + children.
Extended Family
nuclear family + grandparents/aunts/uncles etc.
family එකට grandparents/නෑදෑයෝත් එකතු
If you forget “Extended Family”, write the idea: nuclear family + grandparents/aunts/uncles etc..
Family Life Cycle (FLC)
family buying needs change as the family moves through life stages
family එකේ stage එක වෙනස් වෙද්දි buying needs වෙනස් වෙන එක
If you forget “Family Life Cycle (FLC)”, write the idea: family buying needs change as the family moves through life stages.
Consumer Socialization
children learn how to be consumers
ළමයි shopping/brands ගැන ඉගෙනගන්න process එක
If you forget “Consumer Socialization”, write the idea: children learn how to be consumers.
Socialization Agent
person/group that teaches consumer behavior
consumer behavior ඉගැන්වෙන කෙනා/group එක
If you forget “Socialization Agent”, write the idea: person/group that teaches consumer behavior.
Authoritarian Parenting
strict control, child has little freedom
ගොඩක් strict
If you forget “Authoritarian Parenting”, write the idea: strict control, child has little freedom.
Permissive Parenting
few rules, child has a lot of freedom
rules අඩු
If you forget “Permissive Parenting”, write the idea: few rules, child has a lot of freedom.
Authoritative Parenting
clear rules + explanation + support
rules තියෙනවා, explain කරනවා, support කරනවා
If you forget “Authoritative Parenting”, write the idea: clear rules + explanation + support.
Husband-Dominated Decision
husband has the final say
husband තීරණය කරන එක
If you forget “Husband-Dominated Decision”, write the idea: husband has the final say.
Wife-Dominated Decision
wife has the final say
wife තීරණය කරන එක
If you forget “Wife-Dominated Decision”, write the idea: wife has the final say.
Joint Decision
both decide together
දෙන්නම එකට තීරණය කරන එක
If you forget “Joint Decision”, write the idea: both decide together.
Autonomic Decision
one person decides alone for their own item
එක්කෙනා තමන්ට අදාල දේ තනියම decide කරන එක
If you forget “Autonomic Decision”, write the idea: one person decides alone for their own item.
Children as Three Markets
children are current buyers + influencers + future buyers
දැන් market + parentsට influence + future market
If you forget “Children as Three Markets”, write the idea: children are current buyers + influencers + future buyers.
Initiator
starts the idea of buying
“ගමු” කියලා idea එක මුලින් දාන කෙනා
If you forget “Initiator”, write the idea: starts the idea of buying.
Influencer
changes/affects the decision
decision එකට බලපාන කෙනා
If you forget “Influencer”, write the idea: changes/affects the decision.
Decider
makes the final choice
final decision එක ගන්න කෙනා
If you forget “Decider”, write the idea: makes the final choice.
Buyer
actually pays/buys
buy කරන කෙනා
If you forget “Buyer”, write the idea: actually pays/buys.
User
uses the product
use කරන කෙනා
If you forget “User”, write the idea: uses the product.
Gatekeeper
controls information/access
information හෝ access control කරන කෙනා
If you forget “Gatekeeper”, write the idea: controls information/access.
Social Class
group of people with similar social/economic standing
සමාන සමාජ/ආර්ථික තත්ත්වය තියෙන group එක
If you forget “Social Class”, write the idea: group of people with similar social/economic standing.
Social Stratification
society arranged into higher/lower social layers
සමාජය levels වලට බෙදෙන එක
If you forget “Social Stratification”, write the idea: society arranged into higher/lower social layers.
Social Mobility
moving from one social class to another
එක social class එකකින් වෙන එකකට යන එක
If you forget “Social Mobility”, write the idea: moving from one social class to another.

08. Persuading Consumers

Persuasion
trying to change a person's mind or action
මනුස්සයෙකුගේ හිත/behavior වෙනස් කරවන්න try කරන එක
If you forget “Persuasion”, write the idea: trying to change a person's mind or action.
Communication Model
sender → encode → message → channel → receiver → decode → feedback
company idea එක customerට ගිහින් response එක ආපහු එන flow එක
If you forget “Communication Model”, write the idea: sender → encode → message → channel → receiver → decode → feedback.
Encoding
turn an idea into words, pictures or symbols
idea එක message එකක් කරන එක
If you forget “Encoding”, write the idea: turn an idea into words, pictures or symbols.
Decoding
customer gives meaning to the message
customer message එක තේරුම් ගන්න එක
If you forget “Decoding”, write the idea: customer gives meaning to the message.
Noise
anything that blocks or distracts the message
message එකට බාධා කරන දේ
If you forget “Noise”, write the idea: anything that blocks or distracts the message.
Traditional Media
TV, radio, newspaper; mostly one-way
old media; mostly one-way
If you forget “Traditional Media”, write the idea: TV, radio, newspaper; mostly one-way.
New Media
social/digital media; interactive and targeted
digital; two-way/targeted
If you forget “New Media”, write the idea: social/digital media; interactive and targeted.
Message Framing
say the same fact in a positive or negative way
එකම idea එක good side හෝ bad side විදිහට කියන එක
If you forget “Message Framing”, write the idea: say the same fact in a positive or negative way.
Positive Framing
focus on what the customer gains
ලැබෙන benefit එක emphasize කරන එක
If you forget “Positive Framing”, write the idea: focus on what the customer gains.
Negative Framing
focus on what the customer loses/misses
අහිමි වෙන දේ emphasize කරන එක
If you forget “Negative Framing”, write the idea: focus on what the customer loses/misses.
One-Sided Message
show only the good side
good points විතරක්
If you forget “One-Sided Message”, write the idea: show only the good side.
Two-Sided Message
show good points and some weakness/other side too
good + අඩුපාඩු/other side දෙකම
If you forget “Two-Sided Message”, write the idea: show good points and some weakness/other side too.
Primacy Effect
first information is remembered/stronger
මුලින් දෙන දේ වැඩියෙන් බලපාන එක
If you forget “Primacy Effect”, write the idea: first information is remembered/stronger.
Recency Effect
last information is remembered/stronger
අන්තිමට දෙන දේ වැඩියෙන් බලපාන එක
If you forget “Recency Effect”, write the idea: last information is remembered/stronger.
Comparative Advertising
compare your brand directly or indirectly with competitors
competitor එක්ක compare කරලා ad කරන එක
If you forget “Comparative Advertising”, write the idea: compare your brand directly or indirectly with competitors.
Fear Appeal
use fear of a bad result to motivate action
නරක result එකක් පෙන්නලා action කරවන එක
If you forget “Fear Appeal”, write the idea: use fear of a bad result to motivate action.
Humor Appeal
use fun/jokes to get attention and memory
හිනා/fun use කරන ad
If you forget “Humor Appeal”, write the idea: use fun/jokes to get attention and memory.
Sexual Appeal
use attraction/romance to grab attention
attraction/romance use කරන ad
If you forget “Sexual Appeal”, write the idea: use attraction/romance to grab attention.
Timeliness Appeal
change the message to fit the current time/situation
දැනට තියෙන situation එකට message එක fit කරන එක
If you forget “Timeliness Appeal”, write the idea: change the message to fit the current time/situation.
Psychological Noise
other thoughts in the mind block the message
ඔලුවේ වෙන දේවල් නිසා ad එකට focus නැතිවෙන එක
If you forget “Psychological Noise”, write the idea: other thoughts in the mind block the message.

09. Reference Groups & WOM

Reference Group
people/groups used as a guide for buying and behavior
buy කරන වෙලාවේ බලන/අනුගමනය කරන group එක
If you forget “Reference Group”, write the idea: people/groups used as a guide for buying and behavior.
Source Credibility
how much we trust the information source
source එක කොච්චර trust කරනවද
If you forget “Source Credibility”, write the idea: how much we trust the information source.
Normative Influence
follow the rules/values of a group you belong to
අපි ඉන්න group එකේ rules/values follow කරන එක
If you forget “Normative Influence”, write the idea: follow the rules/values of a group you belong to.
Comparative Influence
compare with people we admire and copy them
අපි admire කරන අයත් එක්ක compare වෙලා copy කරන එක
If you forget “Comparative Influence”, write the idea: compare with people we admire and copy them.
Membership Group
group you belong to or can realistically join
අපි member වෙලා ඉන්න/වෙන්න පුළුවන් group එක
If you forget “Membership Group”, write the idea: group you belong to or can realistically join.
Symbolic Group
group you admire but cannot realistically join
admire කරනවා, but real member වෙන්න බෑ
If you forget “Symbolic Group”, write the idea: group you admire but cannot realistically join.
Friendship Group
friends affect what we buy
friends advice/influence
If you forget “Friendship Group”, write the idea: friends affect what we buy.
Shopping Group
people shop together to reduce risk or enjoy shopping
කට්ටිය එක්ක shopping කරන එක
If you forget “Shopping Group”, write the idea: people shop together to reduce risk or enjoy shopping.
Virtual Community
online group sharing ideas and opinions
online community
If you forget “Virtual Community”, write the idea: online group sharing ideas and opinions.
Advocacy Group
group that protects consumer rights
consumer rights protect කරන group එක
If you forget “Advocacy Group”, write the idea: group that protects consumer rights.
Conformity
change behavior to match a group
group එකට fit වෙන්න behavior වෙනස් කරන එක
If you forget “Conformity”, write the idea: change behavior to match a group.
Opinion Leader
person others ask for product advice
අනිත් අය advice ගන්න expert/influential කෙනා
If you forget “Opinion Leader”, write the idea: person others ask for product advice.
Opinion Leadership
power to influence other consumers' choices
අනිත් අයගේ buying decision වෙනස් කරන්න පුළුවන් බලය
If you forget “Opinion Leadership”, write the idea: power to influence other consumers' choices.
E-WOM
people sharing product opinions online
online word-of-mouth
If you forget “E-WOM”, write the idea: people sharing product opinions online.
Tie Strength
how close the relationship is
source එකත් එක්ක කොච්චර close ද
If you forget “Tie Strength”, write the idea: how close the relationship is.
Viral Marketing
people quickly share a brand message through networks
people share කරලා message එක ඉක්මනට spread කරන එක
If you forget “Viral Marketing”, write the idea: people quickly share a brand message through networks.
Buzz Agent
ordinary consumer used to create talk about a product
normal customer වගේ product එක ගැන talk create කරන කෙනා
If you forget “Buzz Agent”, write the idea: ordinary consumer used to create talk about a product.
Innovators
first people to adopt a new product
අලුත් product එක මුලින්ම ගන්න අය
If you forget “Innovators”, write the idea: first people to adopt a new product.
Early Adopters
buy soon after innovators and often influence others
innovator පස්සේ ඉක්මනට ගන්න influence තියෙන අය
If you forget “Early Adopters”, write the idea: buy soon after innovators and often influence others.
Early Majority
buy before average people but after proof exists
proof ටිකක් ආවට පස්සේ ගන්න අය
If you forget “Early Majority”, write the idea: buy before average people but after proof exists.
Late Majority
buy after most people already use it
ගොඩක් අය ගත්තට පස්සේ ගන්න අය
If you forget “Late Majority”, write the idea: buy after most people already use it.
Laggards
last to adopt or may never adopt
අන්තිමට ගන්න/ගන්නේ නැති අය
If you forget “Laggards”, write the idea: last to adopt or may never adopt.

10. Culture

Culture
shared values, beliefs, rules and ways of life learned by a society
සමාජයක් share කරන values/beliefs/හැසිරීම
If you forget “Culture”, write the idea: shared values, beliefs, rules and ways of life learned by a society.
Cultural Values
ideas about what is important and good in a society
සමාජයට වැදගත් කියලා හිතන දේ
If you forget “Cultural Values”, write the idea: ideas about what is important and good in a society.
Norms
shared rules about normal behavior
සාමාන්‍යයෙන් කරන්න ඕනේ කියන social rules
If you forget “Norms”, write the idea: shared rules about normal behavior.
Customs
common ways people usually do things
සාමාන්‍ය පුරුදු
If you forget “Customs”, write the idea: common ways people usually do things.
Rituals
repeated symbolic actions with cultural meaning
meaning තියෙන නැවත නැවත කරන cultural actions
If you forget “Rituals”, write the idea: repeated symbolic actions with cultural meaning.
Symbols
things/words/signs that carry shared meaning
shared meaning තියෙන sign/thing එක
If you forget “Symbols”, write the idea: things/words/signs that carry shared meaning.
Heroes
people a culture admires and uses as examples
culture එක admire කරන role models
If you forget “Heroes”, write the idea: people a culture admires and uses as examples.
Enculturation
learning your own culture
තමන්ගේ culture එක ඉගෙනගන්න එක
If you forget “Enculturation”, write the idea: learning your own culture.
Acculturation
learning/adapting to another culture
වෙන culture එකකට adapt වෙලා ඉගෙනගන්න එක
If you forget “Acculturation”, write the idea: learning/adapting to another culture.
Cultural Lens
culture changes how you see and judge the world
culture එකෙන් world එක බලන “කන්නාඩිය”
If you forget “Cultural Lens”, write the idea: culture changes how you see and judge the world.
Subculture
smaller cultural group inside a larger culture
ලොකු culture එක ඇතුලේ පොඩි group culture එක
If you forget “Subculture”, write the idea: smaller cultural group inside a larger culture.
Content Analysis
study ads/messages to find cultural values
ads/messages බලලා culture values හොයන එක
If you forget “Content Analysis”, write the idea: study ads/messages to find cultural values.
RVS (Rokeach Value Survey)
survey of important end goals and ways to reach them
values measure කරන survey එක
If you forget “RVS (Rokeach Value Survey)”, write the idea: survey of important end goals and ways to reach them.
LOV (List of Values)
short list used to find a person's most important values
important values ටික තෝරන short survey එක
If you forget “LOV (List of Values)”, write the idea: short list used to find a person's most important values.
VALS
group people by motivation and resources
motivation + resources අනුව people group කරන එක
If you forget “VALS”, write the idea: group people by motivation and resources.
Power Distance
how much unequal power is accepted
boss/authority power gap එක accept කරන level එක
If you forget “Power Distance”, write the idea: how much unequal power is accepted.
Individualism
focus more on self and personal goals
“මම” focus වැඩි
If you forget “Individualism”, write the idea: focus more on self and personal goals.
Collectivism
focus more on group/family goals
“අපි” focus වැඩි
If you forget “Collectivism”, write the idea: focus more on group/family goals.
Uncertainty Avoidance
how uncomfortable people are with unknown/risk
uncertain/risk එකට කොච්චර අකමැතිද
If you forget “Uncertainty Avoidance”, write the idea: how uncomfortable people are with unknown/risk.
Masculinity (Hofstede)
more focus on competition, achievement and success
competition/achievement focus වැඩි culture
If you forget “Masculinity (Hofstede)”, write the idea: more focus on competition, achievement and success.
Femininity (Hofstede)
more focus on care, relationships and quality of life
care/relationship/quality of life focus වැඩි
If you forget “Femininity (Hofstede)”, write the idea: more focus on care, relationships and quality of life.
Long-Term Orientation
focus on future, patience and long-term results
future එක බලලා දිගට plan කරන culture
If you forget “Long-Term Orientation”, write the idea: focus on future, patience and long-term results.
Short-Term Orientation
focus on traditions, present and quick results
present/tradition/quick result focus
If you forget “Short-Term Orientation”, write the idea: focus on traditions, present and quick results.
Indulgence
society allows enjoyment and fun more freely
enjoy/fun කරන්න freedom වැඩි
If you forget “Indulgence”, write the idea: society allows enjoyment and fun more freely.
Restraint
society controls enjoyment with stronger rules
enjoyment control වැඩි
If you forget “Restraint”, write the idea: society controls enjoyment with stronger rules.

11. Brand Positioning — Cross Subject

Brand Positioning
clear place/meaning a brand owns in the customer's mind
customerගේ ඔලුවේ brand එකට clear place එකක් හදන එක
If you forget “Brand Positioning”, write the idea: clear place/meaning a brand owns in the customer's mind.
STP
segment the market → choose target → position the offer
market කඩනවා → group තෝරනවා → mind එකේ place කරනවා
If you forget “STP”, write the idea: segment the market → choose target → position the offer.
Market Segmentation
split a big mixed market into smaller similar groups
ලොකු market එක similar groups වලට කඩන එක
If you forget “Market Segmentation”, write the idea: split a big mixed market into smaller similar groups.
Geographic Segmentation
group people by location
location අනුව group කරන එක
If you forget “Geographic Segmentation”, write the idea: group people by location.
Demographic Segmentation
group by age, income, gender, family etc.
age/income/gender වගේ measurable facts අනුව group කරන එක
If you forget “Demographic Segmentation”, write the idea: group by age, income, gender, family etc..
Psychographic Segmentation
group by lifestyle, personality and inner characteristics
lifestyle/personality අනුව group කරන එක
If you forget “Psychographic Segmentation”, write the idea: group by lifestyle, personality and inner characteristics.
Behavioral Segmentation
group by use, knowledge, attitude or response
use/response/behavior අනුව group කරන එක
If you forget “Behavioral Segmentation”, write the idea: group by use, knowledge, attitude or response.
AIOs
activities + interests + opinions
කරන දේ + කැමති දේ + අදහස්
If you forget “AIOs”, write the idea: activities + interests + opinions.
VALS Framework
segment by motivation and resources
motivation + resources අනුව segment කරන එක
If you forget “VALS Framework”, write the idea: segment by motivation and resources.
Target Market
group the company chooses to serve
company serve කරන්න තෝරන customer group එක
If you forget “Target Market”, write the idea: group the company chooses to serve.
Single Segment Concentration
serve one product-market combination
එක segment එකකට full focus
If you forget “Single Segment Concentration”, write the idea: serve one product-market combination.
Selective Specialization
choose several attractive segments
හොඳ segments කිහිපයක් තෝරන එක
If you forget “Selective Specialization”, write the idea: choose several attractive segments.
Product Specialization
one product for many market groups
එක product එක groups ගොඩකට
If you forget “Product Specialization”, write the idea: one product for many market groups.
Market Specialization
many products for one market group
එක group එකට products ගොඩක්
If you forget “Market Specialization”, write the idea: many products for one market group.
Full Market Coverage
try to serve all major groups
ලොකු market එකේ හැම group එකම cover කරන එක
If you forget “Full Market Coverage”, write the idea: try to serve all major groups.
Mass Marketing
one offer for almost everyone
හැමෝටම එක offer එක
If you forget “Mass Marketing”, write the idea: one offer for almost everyone.
Segment Marketing
different offer for different segments
segment අනුව වෙනස් offer
If you forget “Segment Marketing”, write the idea: different offer for different segments.
Niche Marketing
serve a small special group very well
පොඩි special group එකකට focus
If you forget “Niche Marketing”, write the idea: serve a small special group very well.
Local Marketing
adapt to a local area/community
area එකකට fit කරන marketing
If you forget “Local Marketing”, write the idea: adapt to a local area/community.
Individual Marketing
customize for one person
එක් customer කෙනෙක්ට customize කරන එක
If you forget “Individual Marketing”, write the idea: customize for one person.
Differentiation
create real differences/extra value from competitors
competitorට වඩා real difference එකක් හදන එක
If you forget “Differentiation”, write the idea: create real differences/extra value from competitors.
Frame of Reference
the market/category and competitors customers compare you with
customer ඔයාව compare කරන category/competitors set එක
If you forget “Frame of Reference”, write the idea: the market/category and competitors customers compare you with.
Category Membership
make clear what category the brand belongs to
brand එක මොන category එකේද කියලා clear කරන එක
If you forget “Category Membership”, write the idea: make clear what category the brand belongs to.
Point of Parity (POP)
important things your brand must also have like competitors
category එකේ basic expected similarities
If you forget “Point of Parity (POP)”, write the idea: important things your brand must also have like competitors.
Point of Difference (POD)
strong positive benefit competitors cannot match equally
competitorට same level එකට දෙන්න බැරි unique good point එක
If you forget “Point of Difference (POD)”, write the idea: strong positive benefit competitors cannot match equally.
Competitive Advantage
give customers better value than competitors
competitorට වඩා customerට හොඳ value
If you forget “Competitive Advantage”, write the idea: give customers better value than competitors.
Value Proposition
the main benefit/value promise to the target customer
target customerට දෙන main value promise එක
If you forget “Value Proposition”, write the idea: the main benefit/value promise to the target customer.
VRIO
Value + Rarity + Imitability + Organization check for lasting advantage
resource එක long-term advantage දෙනවද බලන test එක
If you forget “VRIO”, write the idea: Value + Rarity + Imitability + Organization check for lasting advantage.
Perceptual Map
visual map showing where brands sit in customers' minds
customer mind එකේ brands compare කරන map එක
If you forget “Perceptual Map”, write the idea: visual map showing where brands sit in customers' minds.
Positioning Statement
short internal sentence: target + brand + category + difference/reason
target කවුද + brand මොකක්ද + category + unique reason එක sentence එකක
If you forget “Positioning Statement”, write the idea: short internal sentence: target + brand + category + difference/reason.
Slogan
short public line customers remember
customerට මතක හිටින short line එක
If you forget “Slogan”, write the idea: short public line customers remember.
Core Brand Values
most important meanings the brand wants to own
brand එකේ main meanings/values
If you forget “Core Brand Values”, write the idea: most important meanings the brand wants to own.
Brand Mantra
3–5 words that capture the heart of the brand
brand එකේ heart එක වචන 3–5කින්
If you forget “Brand Mantra”, write the idea: 3–5 words that capture the heart of the brand.

Don’t Mix These — පාඩම් අනුව සිංහලෙන්

එක වගේ පේන theories/words ටික lesson-wise බෙදලා, difference එක එක line එකෙන් මතක තියාගන්න හදලා තියෙනවා.

01. Introduction

Production / Product / Selling / Marketing Concept

Production
cheap + available කරන්න
Product
best quality/features හදන්න
Selling
already made product push sell කරන්න
Marketing
customer need first, then product
Memory: cheap / best / push / need-first.

Value / Satisfaction / Retention

Value
money/time/effort වලට වටිනවද?
Satisfaction
performance expectationට match ද?
Retention
customer ආයෙත් එනවද?
Worth it / Happy / Come back.

Segmentation / Targeting / Positioning

Segmentation
market groups වලට බෙදනවා
Targeting
best group තෝරනවා
Positioning
brand එක mind එකේ clear place කරනවා
Split / Pick / Place.

02. Consumer Perception

Sensation / Perception

Sensation
sense organ එකේ immediate raw response
Perception
brain එක ඒ info එකට meaning දෙනවා
Raw feeling / Meaning.

Absolute / JND / Subliminal

Absolute
stimulus එක තියෙනවා කියලා minimum notice
JND
change එකක් minimum notice
Subliminal
conscious awarenessට පහළින් process වෙනවා
Exists / Change / Below awareness.

Selective Exposure / Attention / Defense

Exposure
ad බලනවද skip කරනවද choice
Attention
ad තියෙනවා; relevant එකට focus
Defense
uncomfortable info brain mentally block
Skip / Focus / Brain block.

Figure-Ground / Grouping / Closure

Figure-Ground
main object vs background
Grouping
similar/near items එක group එකක් වගේ
Closure
missing part brain fill කරලා complete
Main / Group / Fill.

Stereotype / Halo

Stereotype
pre-existing category belief එකෙන් judge
Halo
one strong feature එකෙන් whole judgement
Category shortcut / One feature spreads.

03. Consumer Motivation

Innate / Acquired

Innate
born/body need
Acquired
society/culture එකෙන් learned need
Born / Learned.

Intrinsic / Extrinsic

Intrinsic
inner satisfaction
Extrinsic
outside reward/status/avoid punishment
Inside / Outside.

Positive / Negative Goal

Positive
good result toward move
Negative
bad result away move
Toward / Away.

Rational / Emotional Motive

Rational
facts: price/size/performance
Emotional
feelings: pride/love/fear/status
Facts / Feelings.

Substitute Goal / Frustration

Substitute
main goal බැරි නිසා replacement
Frustration
goal blocked නිසා negative feeling
Replace / Blocked feeling.

Behaviorist / Cognitive

Behaviorist
outside stimulus/reward response
Cognitive
inside thinking/beliefs/expectations
Outside response / Inside thinking.

04. Personality

Freudian / Neo-Freudian / Trait

Freudian
hidden unconscious drives
Neo-Freudian
social relationships
Trait
measurable characteristics
Hidden / Social / Measure.

Dogmatism / Innovativeness

Dogmatism
new ideas accept/reject කරන openness
Innovativeness
new products early try willingness
Mind openness / New product speed.

OSL / Sensation Seeking / Variety Seeking

OSL
preferred amount of stimulation/newness
Sensation Seeking
intense novelty + risk
Variety Seeking
satisfied උනත් change/newness
How much / Strong+Risk / Change.

Exploratory / Vicarious / Use Innovativeness

Exploratory
new brand BUY
Vicarious
BUY නෑ, INFO search
Use Innovativeness
current product NEW WAY use
Buy / Search / Re-use.

Visualizer / Verbalizer

Visualizer
photos/video/graphics
Verbalizer
written/verbal detail
Pictures / Words.

Fixated / Compulsive

Fixated
specific category obsession/collecting
Compulsive
buying නවත්තගන්න බැරි
One-category obsession / Can't stop.

05. Consumer Learning

Classical / Operant / Cognitive / Observational

Classical
pair stimuli
Operant
try + consequence
Cognitive
think/search/reason
Observational
watch others
Pair / Try / Think / Watch.

Repetition / Generalization / Discrimination

Repetition
same message repeat
Generalization
same response similar thing
Discrimination
one brand different කියලා learn
Repeat / Similar / Different.

Positive Reinforcement / Negative Reinforcement / Punishment / Extinction

Positive Reinforcement
good reward add → repeat
Negative Reinforcement
bad thing avoid → repeat
Punishment
bad consequence → reduce
Extinction
reward stops → behaviour fades
Good add / Bad avoid / Bad give / Reward gone.

06. Consumer Attitude

Cognitive / Affective / Conative

Cognitive
THINK/KNOW
Affective
FEEL
Conative
ACT/INTEND
Think / Feel / Act.

Toward Object / Toward Behavior / Reasoned Action

Object
thing/brand itself ගැන feeling
Behavior
doing/buying action ගැන feeling
Reasoned Action
own attitude + social pressure → intention
Thing / Doing / Thing+People.

Trying to Consume / Reasoned Action

Trying
success uncertain; obstacles matter
Reasoned Action
beliefs + social pressure → intention/behaviour
Trying with obstacles / Planned action.

Cognitive Dissonance / Attribution

Dissonance
after-buy doubt/regret
Attribution
why did result happen? reason/blame
After-buy doubt / Reason for result.

07. Family & Social Standing

Family / Household

Family
blood/marriage/adoption relation
Household
just people living together
Relation / Shared home.

Husband / Wife / Joint / Autonomic

Husband-dominated
husband final say
Wife-dominated
wife final say
Joint
both decide
Autonomic
one person independently
Final say කාගෙද?

Children as 3 Markets

Primary
child buys now
Influence
child affects parent buying
Future
child becomes future customer
Buy now / Influence now / Buy later.

Influencer / Decider / Buyer / User

Influencer
push/advice
Decider
final choice
Buyer
actually purchase
User
uses product
Advice / Decide / Buy / Use.

08. Persuading Consumers

Traditional / New Media

Traditional
broad, mostly one-way, less targeted
New
interactive, targeted, measurable
One-way broad / Two-way targeted.

Positive / Negative Framing

Positive
gain/save
Negative
loss/miss
Same fact, different angle.

One-Sided / Two-Sided

One-Sided
good points only
Two-Sided
good + limitation/other side
Good only / Good + other side.

Primacy / Recency

Primacy
first info stronger
Recency
last info stronger
First / Last.

Humor / Fear / Sexual / Timeliness

Humor
laugh/like
Fear
worry/bad result
Sexual
attraction/romance
Timeliness
current time/situation fit
Laugh / Worry / Attraction / Right time.

09. Reference Groups & WOM

Normative / Comparative

Normative
belong/acceptance නිසා follow
Comparative
admire/copy benchmark
Belong / Admire.

Membership / Symbolic

Membership
real group
Symbolic
admired dream group
Real / Dream.

Opinion Leader / Celebrity Endorser

Opinion Leader
specific area advice naturally influences
Celebrity
fame used in paid/brand communication
Advice source / Famous promoter.

WOM / E-WOM

WOM
person-to-person talk
E-WOM
online reviews/posts/videos
Talk / Online share.

10. Culture

Enculturation / Acculturation

Enculturation
own culture learn
Acculturation
other culture adapt
Own / Other.

Symbols / Heroes / Rituals / Values

Symbols
